# Anvil code index

Generated by `node head-tools/make-index.js` from `anvil/*.js` (not vendor/, tests/, examples/). Do not edit by hand: rerun it after changing code; `--check` exits 1 when this file is stale.
Use: `grep -n <name> anvil/docs/INDEX.md`, then read that line range of the file. Each line: `name  L<line>  — <comment above it>`.
Namespaced names are exported (UI.x, Kernel.x, Kernel._.x = shared kernel privates, SketchSolver.x, Drawing.x, Drawing._.x, DW.x = UI.drawingInternal.x, Assembly.x …); `(fn)` is the local function name when it differs; `also` = the name is assigned again there (often a wrapper). Bare names are file-private first-level functions; `more:` lines pack the uncommented ones.
Area maps (how things are done, data shapes, traps): `anvil/docs/areas/*.md`.
